# PR #979 rework, round 1 (candidate-49), 2026-11-09 UTC
# W = /home/chris/spark-lab/agents/candidate-49-rework-979-r1 on sp1..sp4 (same path on every node)
# O = /home/chris/spark-lab/agents/candidate-49-own-marks-979 (the own-mark run's workdir; read, not changed)
# Image on every node: spark-ds41f:1107 = sha256:3f32a962ce04b7c8d85337cd427a8946b8b6bcaadd97cc8d8a8cd14e8d68a6ce

# no cluster start/stop. Laptop: reading, editing three Python files in the scratchpad, JSON analysis only.

## 0. Idle checks (from the laptop, before 12:06Z): no ~/spark-hold.json or ~/spark-request.json on sp1..sp4, docker ps empty,
##    image ID as above; each run below re-checks hold file and docker ps on all four nodes once all four GPU locks are held.
for h in sp1 sp2 sp3 sp4; do ssh $h 'hostname; ls ~/spark-hold.json ~/spark-request.json; docker ps; cut -d" " -f1-3 /proc/loadavg; docker image inspect --format "{{.Id}}" spark-ds41f:1107'; done

## 1. Reference code from the image (sp1, no GPU)
docker create --name c49rw979-extract spark-ds41f:1107 true; docker cp c49rw979-extract:/opt/spark-ds41f/candidate/vllm/vllm/distributed $W/img/distributed; docker rm c49rw979-extract
docker run --rm --entrypoint python3 spark-ds41f:1107 -c "import sparknet,os;print(os.path.dirname(sparknet.__file__))"   # a few seconds, CPU only, no --gpus: locates the package
docker create --name c49rw979-extract2 spark-ds41f:1107 true; docker cp c49rw979-extract2:/usr/local/lib/python3.12/dist-packages/sparknet $W/img/sparknet; docker rm c49rw979-extract2
#   read: cuda_communicator.all_gather/all_to_all_rows, pynccl.all_to_all_rows/ring4_relay, sparknet SparknetOneShotAllReduce
#   should_all_gather/should_all_to_all_rows/should_exchange and runtime.topology (TOPOLOGIES = direct, ring4, mesh4);
#   scripts/topology.py: oneshot-ring4 -> ring4 (tp4-ring), oneshot-direct -> direct (tp3-triangle), oneshot-switched -> direct.

## 2. Trees (sp1): the 15 touched files of 0762..0767 from the image, in a git repository
cp $O/patches/*.patch $W/patches/    # sha256 equal to receipts/selection.json (0766 = 1a95ed84...)
docker create --name c49rw979-extract3 spark-ds41f:1107 true
docker cp c49rw979-extract3:/opt/spark-ds41f/candidate/vllm/vllm/models/deepseek_v4_1 $W/img/deepseek_v4_1
while read f; do docker cp c49rw979-extract3:/opt/spark-ds41f/candidate/vllm/$f $W/src/$f; done < $W/touched.txt; docker rm c49rw979-extract3
cd $W/src && git init -b base && git add -A && git commit -m "spark-ds41f-1107 image tree (touched files only)"
for p in 0762 0763 0764 0765; do git am ../patches/$p-*.patch; done; git tag c49-pre0766      # 624df94d82c2
git am ../patches/0766-*.patch; git tag pr979                                                 # the PR's commit, author kept
#   corrected sp_prefill.py and the two test files: edited from pr979's copies in the laptop scratchpad, scp'd to $W/edit/<variant>/
for v in corrA corrB; do git checkout -b $v pr979; cp $W/edit/$v/sp_prefill.py vllm/models/deepseek_v4_1/; cp $W/edit/$v/test_*.py tests/models/;
  git add -A; git commit --amend --no-edit; git am ../patches/0767-*.patch; git diff --check pr979 HEAD~1; done   # clean
git diff --check c49-pre0766 corrA; git diff --check c49-pre0766 corrB                                             # clean
git checkout -b orig pr979 && git am ../patches/0767-*.patch; git checkout -b base0 c49-pre0766 && git am ../patches/0767-*.patch
for arm in corrA corrB orig base0; do git checkout $arm; copy touched.txt files to $W/ov/$arm; done
diff -r $W/ov/orig $O/ov/c49; diff -r $W/ov/base0 $O/ov/no979      # both identical: same arms as the own mark
#   sp_prefill.py sha256: corrA cf8b0302dd38..., corrB cd10236f284a..., orig 58e7f2579b73..., base0 880ff323bde3...
for n in sp2 sp3 sp4; do scp -r $W/ov $W/touched.txt $W/release_tests $n:$W/; done    # overlay tree hash ec94d993117415db on all four
python3 -m py_compile <corrA/corrB sp_prefill.py and test files>                       # ok

## 3. PR #979's own tests on the corrected trees (sp2, under its GPU lock): scripts/runtest.sh, scripts/tests.sh
##    spark-ds41f:1107, the arm's 15 touched files bound read-only over /opt/spark-ds41f/candidate/vllm, tests/conftest.py loaded
##    with the repository's scripts/release_tests/tblib_stub.py (sha256 f5e12613..., as the own mark and release tests do).
cd $W && nohup ./tests.sh corrB corrA > out/tests.log 2>&1 &
#   GPU: pytest -p tblib_stub -v tests/models/test_deepseek_v41_sp_project_reduce_scatter.py tests/models/test_deepseek_v4_1_sp_prefill.py
#   CPU: --gpus all -e CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES= ; pytest -p tblib_stub -v tests/models/test_deepseek_v4_1_sp_prefill.py tests/models/test_deepseek_v4_1_engram.py
#   corrB: GPU 396 passed; CPU 219 passed, 17 skipped.  corrA: GPU 396 passed; CPU 219 passed, 17 skipped.  (exact commands: raw/tests-commands-sp2.txt)

## 4. Bench (sp1 drives sp1..sp4): the own mark's bench_979_integrated.py unchanged (sha256 b05cd7965f53...), its launcher
##    run4.sh / sequence.sh with only the workdir changed, and render.py, which substitutes into the own mark's rendered
##    ring4-c49-boot1 command per node only the container name, MASTER_PORT, overlay dir, bench path and --topology/--arm/--label.
##    Plan pre-registered in raw/plan.txt (12:19:42Z), before the first run.
cd $W && nohup ./sequence.sh ring4-corrA-boot1:corrA:ring4:29641 ring4-corrB-boot1:corrB:ring4:29642 ring4-base0-boot1:base0:ring4:29643 ring4-orig-boot1:orig:ring4:29644 ring4-orig-boot2:orig:ring4:29645 ring4-base0-boot2:base0:ring4:29646 ring4-corrB-boot2:corrB:ring4:29647 ring4-corrA-boot2:corrA:ring4:29648 direct-corrA-boot1:corrA:direct:29649 direct-corrB-boot1:corrB:direct:29650 direct-base0-boot1:base0:direct:29651 direct-orig-boot1:orig:direct:29652 direct-orig-boot2:orig:direct:29653 direct-base0-boot2:base0:direct:29654 direct-corrB-boot2:corrB:direct:29655 direct-corrA-boot2:corrA:direct:29656 > out/sequence.log 2>&1 &
#   12:20:06Z .. 12:34:10Z, every rank exit 0. The first run's sp2 lock waited for this agent's own test job (the corrA CPU tests then ran between runs 1 and 2).
#   A/A appendix, started right after (decided after seeing the main runs; the arm is the baseline, so it cannot favour the candidate):
cd $W && nohup ./sequence.sh ring4-base0-boot3:base0:ring4:29661 ring4-base0-boot4:base0:ring4:29662 direct-base0-boot3:base0:direct:29663 direct-base0-boot4:base0:direct:29664 > out/sequence-aa.log 2>&1 &
#   12:34:33Z .. 12:37:44Z, every rank exit 0.

## 5. Delivered patch (sp1): the measured corrB commit, message rewritten, author and date kept
git checkout -b corrB-final corrB~1 && git commit --amend -F $W/msg-corrB.txt
test "$(git rev-parse HEAD^{tree})" = "$(git rev-parse corrB~1^{tree})"      # same tree as the measured corrB
git format-patch -1 --start-number 766 -o $W/deliver corrB-final               # 0766-perf-dsv41-pipeline-the-sliced-SP-Engram-row-exchang.patch
git worktree add /tmp/c49rw979-verify c49-pre0766 && cd /tmp/c49rw979-verify && git am <that patch> && git am ../patches/0767-*.patch
for f in $(cat $W/touched.txt); do cmp $f $W/ov/corrB/$f; done                 # all 15 equal: 0762..0765 + this 0766 + 0767 = the measured corrB overlay
git worktree remove --force /tmp/c49rw979-verify
git diff pr979 corrB-final > variants/corrected-vs-pr979.diff; git diff c49-pre0766 corrA~1 > variants/corrA-ring4-gated-gather-vs-pre0766.diff; git diff corrA~1 corrB-final > variants/corrA-vs-corrected.diff

## 6. Analysis (laptop, JSON only)
python3 -I scripts/analyze4.py raw > analysis.json && python3 -I scripts/show4.py analysis.json
python3 -I scripts/aa.py raw analysis.json > aa.json

######## raw/plan.txt (pre-registration) ########
# PR #979 rework r1 plan, written 2026-11-09T12:19:42Z before any bench run
Arms (each = spark-ds41f:1107 + the arm overlay of the 7 vLLM files, bound read-only):
  corrA = 0762..0765 + corrected 0766 + 0767 (A: one-shot RoCE gather only on sparknet ring4; one-slice
          project_exchange_rows back to 1107s serial path; sliced pipeline at slices>=2 kept)
          sp_prefill.py sha256 cf8b0302dd38061b7ebfa2677e00805a2c1394111c7c6b3d2ea383153fde2dcf
  corrB = same, but gather_many exactly 1107s grouped PyNCCL (no one-shot gather anywhere)
          sp_prefill.py sha256 cd10236f284af1ed43e1d7a00c74971935cc82864a4729a2925ac811de573f1a
  base0 = candidate-49 without #979 (0762..0765 + 0767), == own-mark no979 (880ff323...)
  orig  = candidate-49 with original #979 (0762..0767), == own-mark c49 (58e7f257...)
Sequence (same session), per topology ring4 then direct, 2 boots per arm, mirrored order:
  corrA corrB base0 orig | orig base0 corrB corrA
Bench: own-mark bench_979_integrated.py unchanged (sha256 b05cd796...): same T, shapes, seeds,
  12 warmup + 40 timed iterations, >=200 ms GPU warm-up before timing.
Judging (pre-registered): per cell (op x T x topology), interval = the two boot medians of an arm;
  "loss" = corrected arm min(boot medians) > baseline max, "gain" = corrected max < baseline min,
  on max-rank medians and on rank0 medians. Mark: project_exchange_rows T>=6144 gain on both
  topologies, bit-exact. Rule: no loss cell vs base0 on max-rank or rank0, except cells where the
  corrected arm and base0 run identical code, which are judged as A/A (identical-code pairs).
Choice corrA vs corrB (pre-registered): corrA only if, on ring4 at the one-shot-routed T (<=1024),
  corrA and orig (identical gather code) are each faster than base0 beyond the interval at >=1 T
  on max-rank and neither is slower beyond the interval at any T on max-rank or rank0;
  otherwise corrB.
# A/A appendix, started 2026-11-09T12:34:33Z right after the main sequence: base0 boots 3 and 4 per topology (same arm, same code as base0 boots 1 and 2), to measure how often identical code reads beyond the two-boot interval
